{"forms":[{"form":"qualities","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The lattice structure of a steel profoundly changes its qualities.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The kind, condition, and/or the set of characteristics of something, as opposed to the quantity of something."],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"While being impulsive can be great for artists, it is not a desirable quality for engineers.","type":"example"},{"text":"Security, stability, and efficiency are good qualities of an operating system.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A single trait."],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"This school is well-known for having teachers of high quality.","type":"example"},{"text":"Quality of life is usually determined by health, education, and income.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Good characteristics in particular; level of excellence; freedom of faults and flaws."],"synonyms":[{"raw_tags":["used in combination"],"tags":["usually"],"word":"duty"}],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"For this building project we need to use quality materials that will last for many decades.","type":"example"},{"text":"That was a quality game by Jim Smith.","type":"example"},{"text":"We had some quality time yesterday!","type":"example"},{"text":"A quality system ensures products meet customer requirements.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Good worth, good make."],"qualifier":"occasionally proscribed","tags":["attributive","countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Position; status; rank."],"tags":["archaic","countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"A peasant is not allowed to fall in love with a lady of quality.","type":"example"},{"text":"Membership of this golf club is limited to those of quality and wealth.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["High social position.
